[Director 指令] **重启恢复 — 状态确认 + 3 张空闲 GPU 填满**

Reviewer 刚做了重启审查，关键信息：
- exp_gemma2b_depth：仍在跑（cuda:0，PID 289968，VocabDiv d=2 阶段 85%），正常
- exp_deberta_code_qwen1b5：状态是 ANALYZING，不是 RUNNING，无 GPU 占用
- cuda:1/2/3 全部空闲
- arXiv DeBERTa 已完成（K*=4），不是 P0 缺口了（ROADMAP 过时）
- 残留进程 PID 175138 (lofo_importance.py) + 8 个 worker 子进程运行 14.5h 已停止，需要 kill 清理

**立即执行（按优先级）：**

**1. 完成 Code DeBERTa 分析（无需 GPU）**
- exp_deberta_code_qwen1b5 在 ANALYZING 状态，读取结果并汇报 K* 和关键指标
- 更新 registry 完成分析

**2. 填满 cuda:1/2/3（用户要求全部卡用上）**
优先级排序：
- **cuda:1**: 7b cross-transfer（llama8b→qwen7b，完成 14/14 矩阵）
- **cuda:2**: exp_olmo_retrain_v2（ecological validity，LoRA retrain chain）
- **cuda:3**: 如有其他高价值 pending 实验就上，否则铺 Pythia retrain chain 或其他 ablation

每个实验启动后立即 `nvidia-smi` 确认 GPU 占用。

**3. 清理残留进程**
- `kill 175138` 及其子进程

**4. 汇报**
- 每个实验的启动确认 + GPU 分配
- Code DeBERTa 分析结果
- Gemma 预计完成时间

验证**: wiki_pythia 2v3 CI_lower=0.6059>0.60, wiki_qwen7b 2v3=0.6223, wiki_llama8b 2v3=0.6258, wiki_mistral7b 2v3=0.6150\\n- **C4 对比**: C4 DeBERTa K*=2 = OBD K*=2（boundary is intrinsic on C4）\\n- 一致\\n- **教训**: 多 config 实验中每个 config 的输出目录必须独立验证，不能假设 pipeline 正确运行了所有 config\\n\\n### Code Cross-Scorer: K*=2 Scorer-Independent (2026-05-19)\\n- **exp_code_qwen1b5_pythia_scorer**: Pythia-1.4B scorer 下 Qwen Code 文本 K*=2（与 Qwen self-scorer 一致）\\n- 1v2 AUC: 0.7197 (Pythia) vs 0.7382 (Qwen)，Δ=-0.019（Pythia 特征提取稍弱但远超 θ=0.60）\\n- 2v3: 0.5308 vs 0.5300，几乎无变化\\n- **结论**: D060 Wikipedia scorer confound 不影响 Code domain（Code 1v2 AUC 远离阈值）\\n- **Scorer confound 影响规律**: 仅在 AUC 极接近 θ 时翻转 K*（Wikipedia 1v2~0.59-0.61），AUC 远离 θ 时 scorer 选择不影响 K* 判定\\n\\n### M011: 实验启动必须 nvidia-smi 验证 (2026-05-19)\\n- **问题**: exp_code_qwen7b 3 次报告\\\"已启动\\\"但实际未运行，cuda:1 空转 9 小时\\n- **根因**: (1) cqa1 无法访问 huggingface.co，datasets.load_dataset 触发 httpx client closed (2) 提交后未验证 GPU 实际占用\\n- **硬规则**: 所有实验提交后必须立即 `nvidia-smi` 确认目标 GPU 有进程 + 显存占用 > 0。如果 0% util / 0 MiB，说明没启动成功，不要回报\\\"已启动\\\"\\n- **修复 pattern**: cqa1 需要 HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com 环境变量；数据文件直接复制而非下载\\n\\n### M012: submit_training_job 必须 bash -c 包裹 conda (2026-05-20)\\n- **问题**: Code DeBERTa 连续 2 次 exit 127 (python not found)\\n- **根因**: tmux 用 sh 执行命令，`source` 和 `conda activate` 是 bash built-in，sh 下不工作\\n- **正确命令**: `bash -c \\\"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python ...\\\"`\\n- **错误命令**: 直接 `HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 python ...`（没有激活 conda，python 不在 PATH）\\n\\n### Pythia Code K*=1 — 跨模型一致性部分否定 (2026-05-19)\\n- **结果**: Pythia-1.4B code domain K*=1 (1v2 AUC=0.5983, 0.17pp below θ=0.60)\\n- 3/5 folds ≥ θ (0.601, 0.600, 0.602)，但 mean 未达标\\n- Qwen Code K*=2 (AUC=0.7382) vs Pythia K*=1 → code K* 不完全 domain-governed\\n- **解释**: Pythia 的 code 能力弱（The Pile 训练，code 比例低），第一代即严重退化\\n- **Framing 影响**: \\\"domain sets ceiling, model competency determines if ceiling is reached\\\"\\n\\n### Retrain-Chain K*=7 跨模型确认 (2026-05-19)\\n- **Qwen2.5-1.5B**: K*≥7, 6v7 AUC=0.641 [0.633, 0.649]\\n- **Pythia-1.4B**: K*=7, 6v7 AUC=0.634 [0.628, 0.641]\\n- 两个架构（GPT-NeoX vs Qwen）retrain K* 完全一致\\n- AUC 衰减形态相似：~0.98 → ~0.63-0.64, 6v7 收敛到相同水平\\n- Pythia 中间 depth AUC 稍高（1v2: 0.947 vs 0.900），可能因更小模型每代漂移更大\\n- **论文 claim**: \\\"retrain K* 由 protocol (LoRA fine-tune on own output) 决定，不由模型架构决定\\\"\\n- **文献支撑**: Bertrand et al. (ICLR 2024) 的理论框架解释了跨模型收敛性\\n\\n### Bootstrap CI 数据泄漏教训 (2026-05-18)\\n- **问题**: retrain-chain 的 bootstrap CI 偏高 (+6.4pp on 1v2)，原因是 bootstrap resampling 未保持 GroupKFold 结构\\n- 重采样打破了 doc-level grouping，导致同一 doc 的不同 depth 出现在 train 和 test 中 → 数据泄漏\\n- **解决**: 废弃 bootstrap CI，使用 fold-based CI (mean ± 1.96 * std / sqrt(n_folds))\\n- **Path B 决策**: 只改 retrain-chain 的 CI 为 fold-based，主 pipeline bootstrap 保留（已验证正确）\\n\\n### Power Curve: n≥2000 Suffices (2026-05-18)\\n- exp_028_power_curve: n=2000 → 100% K*=2 detection, n=1000 → borderline (30-90% depending on seed)\\n- **论文策略 (Director 决策 C)**: 只报 \\\"n≥2000 suffices, we use n=5000\\\"，appendix 放 power_curve.png\\n- 两个 power curve 实验 (exp_028_power_curve vs exp_028_subsampling_power) 在 n=1000 处差 60pp，根因是随机种子不同 + AUC 恰好在 θ 边界。方法学完全一致，10 次 resample 在阈值附近 detection rate 估计本身不稳定\\n- **教训**: 阈值附近的 detection rate 需要 ≥50 resamples 才稳定；远离阈值的 rate 10 次即可\\n\\n### Code Domain (Python) K*=2 — 第 4 域确认 Domain-Governed K* (2026-05-18)\\n- **数据源**: CodeSearchNet Python subset (Husain et al. 2019), 5500 docs, avg 711 chars\\n- **结果 (Qwen2.5-1.5B)**: K*=2, 1v2 AUC=0.7382 [0.709, 0.767], 2v3 AUC=0.5300 [0.514, 0.546]\\n- 1v2 AUC 高于多数 C4 模型 (0.61-0.70)，但 2v3 骤降（JSD 0.044→0.004，10x 衰减）\\n- **4 域全景**: arXiv(3) > C4(2) = Code(2) > Wikipedia(1)\\n- Code 与 C4 共享 K*=2 dominant mode，虽然代码有语法约束但模式重复导致快速坍塌\\n- **注意**: The Stack v2 为 gated dataset，用了开放的 CodeSearchNet 替代。论文入稿须引用 CodeSearchNet\\n- **Bug M010**: run_pipeline.py:462 `int(doc_id)` 对 string ID 崩溃，已 patched\\n\\n### Qwen Retrain-Chain K*≥4 — 第 2 个架构家族确认 (2026-05-18)\\n- Qwen2.5-1.5B LoRA rank=16, C4 5K docs, depth 0-4, self-scoring\\n- **AUC (fold-based)**: 0v1=0.9838, 1v2=0.8969, 2v3=0.8100, 3v4=0.7624 \\n\\n... gen-depth-contamination\\n\\n## 决策与经验\\n\\n### Multi-Source Scorer Confound 三重排除 (2026-05-20)\\n- **结论**: Scorer confound 不影响 multi-source K* 判定，三条独立证据线：\\n  1. **vocab-only** (6 scorer-independent features): v1 K*=2 (1v2=0.676), v2 K*=2 (1v2=0.683)\\n  2. **unified Qwen2.5-1.5B scorer**: mixed K*=2 (1v2=0.7105)，反而高于 confounded (0.7007)\\n  3. **generator label 无增益**: +label K*=2 (1v2=0.7183)，仅 +0.008 AUC\\n- **LLaMA-8B cross-family**: 统一 Qwen scorer 下 LLaMA-8B 单源 K*=1 (1v2=0.6077)，cross-family scoring 更难\\n- **论文策略**: 主表用 unified scorer 结果，appendix 放 vocab-only 作为额外验证\\n\\n### Changepoint K* Framing 指导 (2026-05-20)\\n- **排除 retrain-chain outlier**: 2 个 retrain-chain 实验的 AUC 序列长度和衰减模式与 prompt-chain 完全不同，在 PELT 分析中应排除或单独分析\\n- **Bracket framing**: 论文表述方向为 \\\"K*_CP and K*_θ bracket the discrimination boundary\\\"，不要声称 CP 比 θ 更准\\n- **关键数据**: exact agreement 45.9% (17/37)，within ±1 agreement 94.6% (35/37)，CP 总是 conservative（K*_CP ≤ K*_θ）\\n- **Disagreement AUC range**: [0.607, 0.654]——所有不一致的实验 AUC 都在阈值附近，CP 在 borderline 区域默认保守\\n- **论文用法**: CP 作为 model-free validation，证明 K* 不是 θ=0.60 选择的 artifact\\n\\n### Cron 误判：非 W&B 脚本被标记为 failed (2026-05-19)\\n- **问题**: (cqa1-22115)——均实际成功完成\\n- **修复**: 收到此类 failure 回调时，先 tail_training_logs 查看日志末尾，如有 \\\"SUMMARY\\\" + K* 结果说明实验成功完成\\n- **根治建议**: DeBERTa 脚本应在完成时写一个 sentinel 文件或接入 W&B\\n\\n### Wiki DeBERTa K*=3 — 验证**: wiki_pythia 2v3 CI_lower=0.6059>0.60, wiki_qwen7b 2v3=0.6223, wiki_llama8b 2v3=0.6258, wiki_mistral7b 2v3=0.6150\\n- **C4 对比**: C4 DeBERTa K*=2 = OBD K*=2（boundary is intrinsic on C4）\\n- **含义**: K* boundary 在简单域(C4)是内在的(DeBERTa 也突破不了)，在复杂域(wiki)可被更强分类器部分突破\\n- **OLMo 异常**: K*=1, 1v2 AUC=0.518(近随机)——OLMo wiki 生成质量可能有特殊问题\\n\\n### OBD Baseline Bug: wiki_olmo 目录是 wiki_pythia 的复制品 decay phenomenon.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp_wiki_pythia\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Pythia-1.4B continuation on Wikipedia domain will show K*=2 depth saturation pattern consistent with Qwen2.5-1.5B, demonstrating cross-model generality of the distinguishability decay phenomenon.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp_009_dose_response\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Contamination dose-response: 在 10%/30%/50% synthetic injection 比例下，graduated filtering 相对 binary filtering 的收益是否随 contamination 增加而扩大？验证 C3 (graduated filtering superiority) 在不同 contamination severity 下的 robustness。3 ratios × 3 strategies (no-filter/binary/graduated) × 5 seeds, continued pretraining Qwen2.5-1.5B, eval MMLU+HellaSwag.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_009_dose_50pct_v3\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"50% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 应展现最大优势 >=2pp MMLU（v3: fix OOM, auto batch + offline）。半数数据为合成时 binary 激进丢弃导致数据饥饿。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_009_dose_30pct_v3\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"30% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 优势应 >=1pp MMLU（v3: fix OOM, auto batch + offline）。更高污染率下 binary 丢弃更多可用 gen-1 数据。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_009_dose_10pct_v3\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"10% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 应比 binary 保留 >=0.5pp MMLU（v3: fix OOM, auto batch + offline）。低污染时 gen-1 有训练价值，binary 全丢造成数据损失。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_027_retrain_bootstrap\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Retrain-chain 的 pairwise AUC（0v1 到 3v4）在 10000-iteration bootstrap 95% CI 下均显著高于 θ=0.60，确认 K*≥4 具有统计严格性。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_026_feature_stability_v2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Prompt-chain K*=2 边界对 feature 子集选择 robust：即使从 15D 中随机抽取 5/8/10 维子集，Pythia-1.4B prompt-chain 1v2 AUC（baseline ~0.654）仍 > θ=0.60，证明 K*=2 不依赖特定特征组合。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_026_feature_stability\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"K*=2 对 feature 子集选择 robust：即使从 15D 特征中随机抽取 5/8/10 维子集，1v2 AUC 仍 > θ=0.60，证明 K* 边界不依赖特定特征组合（ad-hoc feature 防御）。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_027\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Document-level bootstrap CI (10000 iterations) on exp-008 v2 retrain-chain data will confirm that all 4 pairwise AUCs have CI_lower > theta=0.60, supporting K*>=4 with statistical significance.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_005_degradation\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"递归生成文本在统计特征空间中呈现单调退化趋势（如 perplexity 上升、词汇多样性下降），且退化速率在 K*=2 后显著减缓，这解释了为什么分类器在深代后失去区分力。\\\"}, \\\"exp_024_arxiv_domain\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Domain ablation: arXiv abstracts 数据下 K*=2 是否成立？验证 K* 跨数据域（C4 vs Wikipedia vs arXiv）的稳定性。Qwen2.5-1.5B base, continuation, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, 5K docs, depth 0-5。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_017_qwen_continuation\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Continuation mode 的 K* 应等于 rewrite mode（K*=2），因为 K* 饱和边界是递归生成的文本固有属性，与生成模式（续写 vs 改写）无关——两种模式在深代时都收敛到相同的统计吸引子。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_012_binary_confidence\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Binary detector confidence（DivEye log-PPL、Binoculars ratio）与 true generational depth 相关性弱（Spearman rho < 0.3），因为这些检测器将所有合成文本折叠为单一类别，无法区分 gen-1 和 gen-3+，暴露 binary 范式的信息损失。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_C_permutation_10k\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"10K permutation resamples 不应改变任何 K* 结论（所有 p < 0.01 的 pair 仍应 p < 0.01），确认 500 resamples 已有足够精度，K*=2 结论的统计基础稳固。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_002_olmo_depth\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"exp_009_dose_30pct_v2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"30% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 优势应 >=1pp MMLU（v2: fix HF mirror）。机制同 v1：更高污染率下 binary 丢弃更多可用 gen-1 数据。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp_009_dose_10pct_v2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"10% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 应比 binary 保留 >=0.5pp MMLU（v2: fix HF mirror）。机制同 v1：低污染时 gen-1 有训练价值，binary 全丢造成数据损失。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp_009_dose_50pct\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"50% synthetic injection 下，graduated filtering 应展现最大优势（>=2pp MMLU），因为半数数据为合成时 binary filtering 的激进丢弃导致严重数据饥饿，而 graduated 保留 gen-1 维持训练集规模。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp_009_dose_30pct\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"30% synthetic injection 下，graduated filtering 优势应大于 10% 场景（>=1pp MMLU），因为更高污染率意味着 binary filtering 丢弃更多可用 gen-1 数据，而 graduated 仅移除 gen-2+ 有害部分。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp_009_dose_10pct\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"10% synthetic injection 下，graduated filtering 应比 binary filtering 保留更多 MMLU 性能（>=0.5pp），因为低污染场景中 gen-1 文本仍有训练价值，binary 全部丢弃造成不必要的数据损失。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp_kstar_theta\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"K*(θ) 曲线在 θ∈[0.55,0.65] 存在 plateau（K* 对阈值不敏感），且 Cohen's h 在 depth=K* 到 depth=K*+1 间降至 < 0.2（小效应量），为 θ=0.60 提供非任意性理论依据。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_022_cross_scorer_matrix\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Self-scoring（generator=scorer）的 K* 和 pairwise AUC 应与 cross-scoring（generator≠scorer）差异 < 0.02，证明 K*=2 不是 scorer-generator familiarity artifact，而是文本本身的可区分性边界。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_008_retrain_chain_v2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Retrain-chain prompt-chain 是 conservative upper bound。C4 5K docs, depth 0-4, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp-008-retrain-chain\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Retrain-chain ecological validity: Pythia-1.4B LoRA (rank=16, alpha=32) retrain-chain K* ≤ prompt-chain K*=2. 每代独立 LoRA fine-tune 1 epoch on 前代输出，再用 fine-tuned model 生成下一代。验证 prompt-chain 是 conservative upper bound。C4 5K docs, depth 0-4, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"failed\\\"}, \\\"exp_025_pythia_arxiv\\\": artifact。Pythia-1.4B continuation, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, 2200 docs, depth 0-5, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B base。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_020_pythia_decoding\\\": {\\\"status\\\": continuation, C4 5K, depth 0-5。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_018_llama8b_depth\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Cross-family K* 验证：LLaMA-3.1-8B continuation mode 的 K* 是否 = 2？首次超出 Qwen 家族，验证 K* universality 跨模型架构。C4 5K, depth 0-5, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B base。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_023_domain_ablation\\\": 0-5。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_019b_mistral_cross_scorer\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"排除 self-scorer 因素：Mistral-7B depth chain 用 Qwen2.5-1.5B-base 评分后 K* 是否=2？如果 K*=2 则 exp-019 的 K*=1 是 self-scorer artifact；如果 K*=1 则是 Mistral 生成文本的固有特性。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_019_mistral7b_depth\\\": {\\\"status\\\": self-scoring 方法一致，exp_A_alt_scorer 已证明 scorer 不影响 K*。\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_016_base_vs_instruct\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Base vs \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_003_decoding_ablation\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"解码策略调控 K*：不同 top_p/temperature 设置是否改变饱和边界（greedy→更低 K*? 高温度→更高 K*?）\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_004_7b_crossscale\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"K*=2 在 7B scale 下是否成立？Qwen2.5-7B continuation mode, C4 5K seeds, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B。Reviewer R2 Critical: 现有实验仅 ≤1.5B\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_E_paired_stats\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"C3 filtering 策略间差异是否统计显著？Holm-Bonferroni 多重比较校正 + Cohen's d 效应量（FR-5 Warning）\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_D_obd_n500\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"OBD-LightGBM 在 N=500 子集上 accuracy 是否仍高于 LLM baselines？验证样本量匹配下的公平比较（FR-12 Warning）\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_B_14d_ablation\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"移除 self_BLEU（14D vs 15D）后 K*=2 是否稳定？排除 self_BLEU information leakage（FR-3 Critical）\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_A_alt_scorer\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"替代 scorer (Pythia-1.4B, OLMo-1B) 计算 surprisal features 时，K*=2 是否仍成立？验证 K* 不依赖 scorer-generator 匹配（FR-2 Critical）\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_015_neural_probe\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Learned representations (frozen LLM → MLP) 的 K* 是否高于 hand-crafted 15D features？若 K*_neural > K*_15D，说明 15D projection 是 K*=2 的部分原因；若相同，加强 information-theoretic impossibility 论证\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_006_graduated_filtering\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"K*-aware graduated filtering (exploiting human/gen-1/gen-2+ trichotomy) yields better downstream LM quality than binary filtering on MMLU and HellaSwag. 6 strategies × 5 seeds × α sweep.\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_007_bootstrap_ci\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"K* 统计严谨性验证：bootstrap 95% CI 和 permutation test 确认 K*=2 在三个模型族上统计显著\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_011_llm_zeroshot\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Frontier LLMs (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.6) cannot reliably estimate generational depth via zero-shot prompting, motivating the need for specialized statistical methods\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_013_cross_transfer\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Depth signal 是文本固有属性还是 model self-familiarity artifact？4×4 cross-model transfer matrix 测试分类器跨模型泛化能力\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_010_ordinal_baseline\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Ordinal structure improves depth classification: ordinal methods should outperform unordered multiclass on MAE\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_001_pythia_depth\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"K* 跨模型一致性验证：Pythia-1.4B 在相同种子数据和解码策略下的 K* 是否与 Qwen2.5-1.5B 一致（K*=2）\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"mvp_pilot_sat_v1\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"递归生成文本在统计特征空间上存在可分辨的代际差异，且差异随深度呈系统性趋势（K* ≥ 3）\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}},       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"experiments\": {\"exp_deberta_code_qwen1b5\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"DeBERTa supervised classifier on Code domain yields K*=2 (matching OBD K*=2), confirming representation-independent domain ceiling holds for Code domain (C4 K*=2, wiki K*=3, arXiv K*=3, code K*=2).\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_gemma2b_depth\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Gemma-2-2B (6th architecture family) yields K*=2 on C4 web text under continuation mode, consistent with domain ceiling K*=2 observed across 5 other families (Qwen/Pythia/OLMo/LLaMA/Mistral), strengthening the claim that C4 ceiling is architecture-independent.\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\"}, \"exp_multi_source_arxiv\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Multi-source OBD K* on arXiv domain preserves domain ceiling K*=3 when mixing 3 generators (Qwen/Pythia/LLaMA-8B) with unified Qwen2.5-1.5B scorer, consistent with single-source arXiv K*=3 and higher than C4 multi-source K*=2, confirming domain hierarchy in multi-source setting.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_multi_source_detection\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"OBD K* is preserved when training on a mixed pool of outputs from 3 different generators (Qwen-1.5B, Pythia-1.4B, OLMo-1B), and adding generator identity as an extra feature does not improve depth discrimination.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_changepoint_kstar\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Non-parametric changepoint detection (PELT) on pairwise AUC sequences should recover K* consistent with the fixed θ=0.60 threshold, validating that K* is not an artifact of threshold choice.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_cross_transfer_llama8b_to_qwen7b_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Cross-model transfer: DeBERTa trained on LLaMA-8B wiki depth data can discriminate Qwen-7B wiki depth boundaries (K* transfer). Rerun of exp_cross_transfer_llama8b_to_qwen7b which crashed due to --gpu 1 device error.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_arxiv_qwen1b5\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"DeBERTa supervised classifier on arXiv domain yields K*=3 (matching OBD K*=3), confirming representation-independent domain ceiling holds across all 4 domains (C4 K*=2, wiki K*=3, code K*=2, arXiv K*=3).\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_cross_transfer_llama8b_to_qwen7b\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Cross-model transfer: DeBERTa trained on LLaMA-8B wiki depth data can discriminate Qwen-7B wiki depth boundaries (K* transfer), testing whether depth features are model-agnostic within wiki domain.\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\"}, \"exp_cross_transfer_qwen7b_to_llama8b\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Cross-model transfer: DeBERTa trained on Qwen-7B wiki depth data can discriminate LLaMA-8B wiki depth boundaries (K* transfer), testing whether depth features are model-agnostic within wiki domain.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_cross_transfer_gpu3\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Sub-experiment of exp_deberta_cross_transfer_wiki: GPU3 ran olmo↔llama8b, pythia↔mistral7b pairs (mistral7b pairs were bonus, qwen7b↔llama8b missing).\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_cross_transfer_gpu2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Sub-experiment of exp_deberta_cross_transfer_wiki: GPU2 ran olmo↔qwen7b, pythia→llama8b, llama8b→pythia pairs.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_cross_transfer_gpu1\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Sub-experiment of exp_deberta_cross_transfer_wiki: GPU1 ran pythia↔olmo and pythia↔qwen7b pairs.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_cross_transfer_wiki\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"DeBERTa cross-model transfer on wiki domain: if off-diagonal retention is high (>90%), depth signals are model-family invariant even with neural features; if wiki retention exceeds C4 retention, domain complexity affects transfer more than feature representation.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_c4_1b5_qwen_cont\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"C4 1.5B DeBERTa qwen_cont256 config (length-256 continuation): if K*=2, confirms boundary holds across generation strategies (base vs continuation) at 1.5B scale.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_c4_1b5_pythia\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"C4 1.5B DeBERTa pythia_greedy config: apple-to-apple comparison with OBD Table 2. If K*=2 consistent with qwen_rw, confirms boundary is generator-invariant at 1.5B scale on C4.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_32b_c4_6class\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"32B DeBERTa 6-class rerun (depth 0-5) to fill 4v5 pair in paper table. If K* remains 2, confirms boundary even with full depth range; 4v5 AUC provides additional evidence of signal decay pattern.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_c4_1b5\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"C4 1.5B DeBERTa apple-to-apple comparison with Table 2 OBD results: if DeBERTa K*=2 on same 1.5B C4 data as OBD, confirms boundary is intrinsic; if K*>2, quantifies neural vs handcrafted gap at 1.5B scale.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_upperbound\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"DeBERTa-v3-base fine-tuned ordinal classifier as upper-bound baseline for 15D handcrafted features: if DeBERTa also yields K*=2 on C4 depth chains, the saturation boundary is near-intrinsic to generation process rather than a feature ceiling; if K*=3+, quantifies the gap between handcrafted and neural representations. Uses same 5-fold GroupKFold, pairwise AUC evaluation, θ=0.60 K* criterion.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_32b_c4\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Scale-dependent K* on C4: Qwen2.5-32B fp16 continuation on C4 web text 的 K* 是否仍 = 2？验证 K*=2 dominant mode 在 32B scale 是否稳定（已确认 1.5B/7B/8B/14B 均 K*=2），排除 scale ceiling effect。5000 docs, depth 0-5, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_deberta_c4\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"DeBERTa-v3-base fine-tuned on raw text provides an upper-bound for C4 depth distinguishability compared to 15D LightGBM on handcrafted features. Pairwise AUC should exceed LightGBM at all depth pairs since the model has access to full lexical/semantic signal.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_code_qwen7b\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Qwen2.5-7B code domain K* measurement: Qwen family scale invariance test on CodeSearchNet Python. Pythia Code K*=1 弱化了跨模型一致性。Qwen-7B 测试两个方向：(1) Qwen 家族内 Code 的 scale invariance（1.5B K*=2, 7B K*=?），(2) 若 LLaMA/Mistral 也 K*=1，Qwen-7B K*=2 能维持 \\\"code-competent models reach K*=2\\\" 叙事。5000 docs, depth 0-5, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_code_mistral7b\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Mistral-7B code domain K* measurement: Mistral-7B-v0.3 continuation on CodeSearchNet Python subset. Mistral 是唯一展现极端 domain dependence 的模型（C4 K*=1, arXiv K*=3）。若 code K*=2（与 Qwen/LLaMA 一致）→ code K* domain-governed, Mistral one-wash 仅限 C4。若 code K*=1 → one-wash 是 model-intrinsic property for non-academic text。5000 docs, depth 0-5, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_code_llama8b\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Code domain (Python) cross-model K* validation: LLaMA-3.1-8B continuation on CodeSearchNet Python subset (Husain et al. 2019). 验证 code domain K* 跨模型一致性（与 exp_code_qwen1b5 对比）。如果 K* 一致，确认 code domain K* 是 domain-governed 而非 model-specific。5500 docs, depth 0-5, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_code_qwen1b5_pythia_scorer\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Code domain scorer confound control: Qwen-1.5B Code K*=2 uses same-family Qwen scorer. D060 Wikipedia showed Qwen scorer inflates AUC by +0.005~0.027. If Pythia-1.4B scorer yields K*=2 on same Qwen Code text, confirms K*=2 is text-intrinsic (not scorer artifact). If K*=1, Qwen Code K*=2 is partially scorer-driven, weakening domain ceiling claim. Cross-family scorer validation on existing exp_code_qwen1b5 depth 0-5 data.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_pythia_retrain_depth7\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Pythia-1.4B retrain-chain depth extension to 7: 验证 retrain-chain K* >> prompt-chain K* 是否跨模型家族一致（Qwen retrain K*≥7）。如果 Pythia 也 K*≥5-7，confirms retrain artifacts are model-independent。C4 5K docs, LoRA rank=16 alpha=32, depth 0-7。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_qwen_retrain_depth7\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Qwen2.5-1.5B retrain-chain extended to depth 7: K* measurement at higher depths. C4 5K docs, depth 0-7, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B (self-scoring). Extends exp_qwen_retrain_chain (depth 0-4).\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_code_pythia1b4\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Pythia-1.4B on Code domain (Python/CodeSearchNet) will show K*=2 consistent with Qwen code domain results, indicating generalization depth saturation is model-independent for code data.\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\"}, \"exp_028_power_curve\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Subsampling power analysis: K*=2 判定在 C4 上所需的最低样本量。在 n={500,1000,2000,3000,4000,5000} 下 10 次 random resample，每次 5-fold CV 计算 1v2 AUC。输出 power curve（K*=2 判定成功率 vs n）。验证类实验，回应 Reviewer 对 statistical power / sample size adequacy 的质疑。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_qwen_retrain_chain\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Qwen2.5-1.5B retrain-chain ecological validity: LoRA rank=16, alpha=32, 1 epoch per generation. Retrain-chain K* ≤ prompt-chain K*=2, confirming prompt-chain as conservative upper bound across a second architecture family (Qwen vs Pythia). C4 5K docs, depth 0-4, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B (self-scoring).\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_code_qwen1b5\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Code domain (Python) K* measurement: Qwen2.5-1.5B continuation on CodeSearchNet Python subset (Husain et al. 2019). Code 的高度结构化特性（语法约束、缩进、函数签名）应使代际退化慢于 C4 (K*≥2)，可能接近 arXiv (K*=3)。5500 docs, depth 0-5, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_olmo_retrain_chain\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"OLMo-1B retrain-chain (LoRA rank=16, 1 epoch per generation) on C4 produces K* ≤ 2, confirming prompt-chain as conservative upper bound for K*.\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\"}, \"exp_028_subsampling_power\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"C4 K*=2 信号的 minimum sample size: 在 n={1000..5000} 范围内，GroupKFold(5) 1v2 AUC 是否稳定超过 θ=0.60？确定可靠判定 K*=2 所需的最小样本量。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_026_arxiv_filtering\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Graduated depth weighting (α=0.9^d) on arXiv depth chains will outperform both discarding data entirely.\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\"}, \"exp_027_latex_ablation\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"arXiv K*=3 不依赖 LaTeX 格式特征。去除 LaTeX markup 后重新提取 15D features 并跑 \"conclusion\": \"negative\"}, \"exp_14b_c4\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Scale-dependent K* on C4: Qwen2.5-14B fp16 continuation on C4 web text 的 K* 是否仍 = 2？验证 K*=2 dominant mode 在 14B \"50% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 应展现最大优势 >=2pp MMLU（v3: fix OOM, auto batch + offline）。半数数据为合成时 binary 激进丢弃导致数据饥饿。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_009_dose_30pct_v3\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"30% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 优势应 >=1pp MMLU（v3: fix OOM, auto batch + offline）。更高污染率下 binary 丢弃更多可用 gen-1 数据。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_009_dose_10pct_v3\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"10% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 应比 binary 保留 >=0.5pp MMLU（v3: fix OOM, auto batch + offline）。低污染时 gen-1 有训练价值，binary 全丢造成数据损失。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_027_retrain_bootstrap\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Retrain-chain 的 pairwise AUC（0v1 到 3v4）在 10000-iteration bootstrap 95% CI 下均显著高于 \"Document-level bootstrap CI (10000 iterations) on exp-008 v2 retrain-chain data will confirm that all 4 pairwise AUCs have CI_lower > theta=0.60, supporting K*>=4 with statistical significance.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_005_degradation\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"hypothesis\": \"递归生成文本在统计特征空间中呈现单调退化趋势（如 perplexity 上升、词汇多样性下降），且退化速率在 K*=2 base, continuation, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, 5K docs, depth 0-5。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_017_qwen_continuation\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Continuation mode 的 K* 应等于 detector confidence（DivEye log-PPL、Binoculars ratio）与 true generational depth 相关性弱（Spearman rho < 0.3），因为这些检测器将所有合成文本折叠为单一类别，无法区分 gen-1 和 gen-3+，暴露 binary 范式的信息损失。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, 应展现最大优势（>=2pp MMLU），因为半数数据为合成时 binary filtering 的激进丢弃导致严重数据饥饿，而 graduated 保留 gen-1 维持训练集规模。\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\"}, \"exp_009_dose_30pct\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"30% \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_022_cross_scorer_matrix\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Self-scoring（generator=scorer）的 K* 和 pairwise AUC 应与 cross-scoring（generator≠scorer）差异 < 0.02，证明 upper bound。C4 5K docs, depth 0-4, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, scorer=Qwen2.5-1.5B.\", \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp-008-retrain-chain\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"hypothesis\": \"Retrain-chain 22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
```text
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
```

</details>

AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained('allenai/OLMo-1B', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache')\"\n     ```\n     如果 HF 下载失败，尝试 ModelScope：\n     ```\n     pip install modelscope 2>/dev/null\n     python -c \"from modelscope import snapshot_download; snapshot_download('allenai/OLMo-1B', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache')\"\n     ```\n   - 如果两种方式都失败，汇报失败原因\n\n2. **检查已有 retrain chain 脚本**\n   - 查看 /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/scripts/ 下是否有 retrain chain 相关脚本\n   - 参考 exp008_retrain_chain.py（Pythia 版本）或 exp_qwen_retrain_chain.py（Qwen 版本）\n   - 如果有 OLMo 版本的脚本，检查是否需要修改\n\n3. **准备 OLMo retrain chain 脚本**\n   如果没有现成的 OLMo 版本：\n   - 基于已有的 retrain chain 脚本（优先 Qwen 版本因为更新），修改为 OLMo-1B\n   - 关键修改点：模型名改为 allenai/OLMo-1B，确保 tokenizer 兼容\n   - 保存到 /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/scripts/exp_olmo_retrain_v3.py\n   - 文件顶部写注释说明：文件功能、输入输出、关键依赖\n\n4. **Dry-run 验证**\n   ```\n   CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2 python scripts/exp_olmo_retrain_v3.py --help 2>&1 | head -20\n   ```\n   或者跑第一个 step 的前几行验证能启动。\n\n5. **汇报**\n   - OLMo 模型是否可用\n   - 脚本路径和关键参数\n   - 是否 dry-run 通过\n   - 预计运行时间\n   - 如果有阻塞问题，详细描述\n\n**不要提交训练任务**——只做准备和验证，提交由 Agent 来做。",
